HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-8","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_03887c878173387551dd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:2:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Bilme isteği hedefe yönelmiş olsa da sonraki esenlik, bu yönelişin baskın ya da saldırgan bir ele geçirme olmadığını düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0910eb162aa6ca117a86","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:2:5\",\"root_001205\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kadir'in gücü soyut bir tanımla değil, izinli inişin ve her işi kapsayan düzenin neler gerçekleştirdiğiyle bilinir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0f535a9fdebac8f2b1c2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:2:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bu geceye ilişkin bilgi kendiliğinden ele geçirilen değil, bildirim ve izinle açılan bir bilgidir; soru bir erişim eşiği kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1590c3d3f7126a6fcc54","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:2:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Soru yalnız gecenin bilinmezliğini bildirmez; sonraki ayetlerin karşılaştırma, işleyiş ve sınır yoluyla vereceği bilgiyi başlatan bir öğretme eylemidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_18f7a8ae716f21b9a9a3","text":"3. **A measure known by the failure of an ordinary scale.** **ق د ر B001** supplies measure, extent, and limit. The first answer, 97:3, introduces the exact count *أَلْف* (**ء ل ف B001**) of calendrical units *شَهْر* (**ش ه ر B001**), but says the single night is *خَيْرٌ مِّنْ* that span. I infer that the thousand-month scale is offered as a measuring instrument and simultaneously shown inadequate by the comparative. Thus the question \"what is the night of measure?\" is answered by a measure that the night exceeds, keeping calculability and excess active together.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3e56624162fcb93c20cb","text":"4. **Capacity becomes knowable through its operation.** **ق د ر B003** is capacity reaching effective control. Rather than define that capacity abstractly, 97:4 displays descending agents, authorization from their Lord, and scope over *كُلِّ أَمْرٍ*. The packet states the participants and authority relation; I infer that these are evidence of what the night's capacity can effect. The question consequently asks for practical recognition of power through an enacted system, not only lexical recognition of a title.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4fd6055d08cb2588695e","text":"3. **A pointed inquiry cannot force the opening.** Lexical evidence: `د ر ي` B004, sharp horn/comb-like implement; activated by `ف ج ر` B001, wide splitting/outflow, and `ط ل ع` B001/B003, rising and disclosure. The packet supplies a root with a sharp implement branch, plus dawn/splitting and disclosure; I supply, as an exploratory arrow, that human inquiry may point toward the matter but the actual opening comes from the dawn/disclosure sequence. Reading change: the ayah reads as disciplined epistemic humility: the measure is not pried open by the addressee's instrument.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5d69adf3e76a9c5c117b","text":"97:3 first answered the question with a conventional measure that the night exceeds, so **ق د ر B001** became both measurable and resistant to its proposed scale. 97:4 then supplied an operational rather than definitional answer through capacity, permission, and every affair. Finally, 97:5's **ط ل ع B003** disclosure and **ف ج ر B002** break from darkness revealed that the question's delayed pedagogy formally imitates the night-to-dawn movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6b351278790220655198","text":"4. **Knowing is aimed at a target but not a raid.** Lexical evidence: `د ر ي` B002, aiming at/setting upon something especially in a raid; activated contrastively by `س ل م` B004 in 97:5, peace as opposite of war, and `ن ز ل` B007 in the inventory, coming down to fight, which is present for the same descent root in the packet. The packet supplies the aim/raid branch, later peace, and repeated descent; I supply the negative mechanism that the approach to qadr-night is an aimed access without hostile seizure. Reading change: the question holds desire for access while refusing conquest as the mode of knowing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_735055c5d54fb2a67200","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:2:5\",\"root_001205\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yanıtın değerden işleyişe, oradan süre ve sınıra ilerleyen sırası, Kadir'deki planlı düzeni kendi yapısında yansıtır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_75a1eb3d158c518ec0ab","text":"1. **The question as an act of causing knowledge.** **د ر ي B001** covers knowing, awareness, and causing another to know. The causative *أَدْرَىٰكَ* is enclosed by two occurrences of *مَا*, and the three following ayat supply, in order, a comparison, an interior operation, and a temporal limit. The packet gives this question-answer sequence; I infer that the question is itself the first stage of making the addressee know by preparing those relations. The ayah therefore performs instruction rather than merely declaring the night unknowable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7d495f7e0608d4b4f142","text":"2. **Knowledge staged as movement from concealment to disclosure.** The fixed **ل ي ل B001** supplies darkness, while **د ر ي B001** supplies awareness. The answer remains deferred through the dark interval until 97:5's *مَطْلَعِ* can activate **ط ل ع B003**, disclosing or looking into a matter, alongside **ف ج ر B002**, dawn breaking from night. The packet supplies the temporal progression; I infer its epistemic arrow from hidden to disclosed. The question then reproduces the night's own form: it withholds first and lets understanding emerge at the boundary.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_820d65582714c992429a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:2:5\",\"root_001205\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gecenin ölçüsü, bin aylık olağan ölçeğin sınanıp yetersiz kalmasıyla öğretilir; böylece gece hem ölçülebilir hem de sunulan ölçüyü aşar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000022/B003","root_000473/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_96a15280bfdb1bc6bd2d","text":"1. **Knowledge requires an authorized disclosure.** Lexical evidence: `د ر ي` B001, knowing and causing another to know; activated by `ء ذ ن` B003 in 97:4, public notification/knowing by announcement, and `ط ل ع` B003 in 97:5, disclosure of a matter. The packet supplies the interrogative \"what made you know?\" plus later notification and disclosure branches; I supply the arrow that knowledge of this night is not self-generated but granted through an announced opening. Reading change: the question reads as a threshold of access, not merely a rhetorical intensifier.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000022/B003","root_000473/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_986d79bd85e9d16fc0b3","text":"97:5 supplied the strongest later surprise: `ط ل ع` B003/B006 and `ف ج ر` B001 made the knowledge-question in 97:2 feel like an epistemic lock awaiting an authorized opening. 97:4 also changed the force of \"أَدْرَىٰكَ\" by adding `ء ذ ن` B003/B004: knowing is tied to announcement and permission, not mere curiosity. The exploratory pointed-instrument branch `د ر ي` B004 became more useful only after the dawn-splitting branch appeared, because it clarified what inquiry cannot itself do.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b0af146daaf8dfc3731c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:2:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Sorgulamanın sivri bir araç gibi konuya yönelmesi, gizliyi kendi gücüyle yarıp açamaz; asıl açılış şafakla gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001","root_000473/B003","root_001392/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cf47c6b262f9a02226e5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:2:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"97:2:4\",\"root_001392\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Soru, karanlıkta gizlenen geceyi hemen ele vermez; bilme, şafaktaki görünür açılışa kadar aşamalı olarak gelişir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B003","root_001205/B001","root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f9a28f40597eb734b64d","text":"2. **The hidden night resists direct grasp.** Lexical evidence: `د ر ي` B003, stealth and concealment in hunting; `ل ي ل` B001, darkness; `ق د ر` B001, limit/measure. Activated by 97:5's `ط ل ع` B008/B009, repeated peering and visible appearance. The packet supplies darkness, measure, a knowledge-question, and later looking/appearance branches; I supply the mechanism that the reader approaches a concealed object whose visibility comes only by staged emergence. Reading change: \"أَدْرَىٰكَ\" becomes a scene of seeking a hidden thing under night, not an immediate possession of information.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fa71b18450c13da0f866","text":"5. **Order becomes knowable through an ordered answer.** **ق د ر B005** supplies planning and arrangement by deliberation. The answers move from value (97:3), to authorized process (97:4), to duration and endpoint (97:5); within the process, **ء م ر B001/B002** supplies affairs and directives. I infer that the answer's own sequence mirrors the planned ordering latent in *qadr*. The fixed ayah therefore opens an inquiry whose response is architected like the ordered event it describes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
